## LLM Infrastructure

### [v0.27.1](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/releases/tag/v0.27.1)

**来源**: vLLM GitHub Releases

**摘要**: <p>This is a patch release on top of v0.27.0.</p>
<ul>
<li>Support quantized DSpark Markov heads (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50424">#50424</a>)</li>
</ul>

---

### [v0.27.0](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/releases/tag/v0.27.0)

**来源**: vLLM GitHub Releases

**摘要**: <h1>vLLM v0.27.0 Release Notes</h1>
<h2>Highlights</h2>
<p>This release features 561 commits from 242 contributors (64 new)!</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>Kimi K3 support</strong> with a full stack landing in one release: core model files and kernels (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50089">#50089</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50000">#50000</a>), Python (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50093">#50093</a>) and Rust (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50104">#50104</a>) frontends, AttnRes kernels (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50090">#50090</a>), DeepGEMM support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50458">#50458</a>), compressed-tensors quantized checkpoints (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50500">#50500</a>), DSpark AR fusion (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50242">#50242</a>), and an option to shard the shared expert instead of replicating it (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50656">#50656</a>).</li>
<li><strong>More new models</strong>: Qwen3.5 text-only dense and MoE models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50210">#50210</a>) with EVS video token pruning (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48912">#48912</a>), K-EXAONE-2.0-750B-A37B (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50524">#50524</a>), VaultGemma via the Transformers modeling backend (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49803">#49803</a>), and jina-embeddings-v5-text-nano (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50688">#50688</a>).</li>
<li><strong>PyTorch 2.13.0 upgrade</strong> along with torchvision 0.28.0 and Triton 3.7.1 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48155">#48155</a>) — this is a breaking environment change; XPU (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48677">#48677</a>) and CPU (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50412">#50412</a>) followed to torch 2.13 as well.</li>
<li><strong>FlashAttention 4 integration deepens on SM100</strong>: FP8 KV cache support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/42569">#42569</a>) and headdim-256 support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/42669">#42669</a>), backed by a new JIT warmup infrastructure (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47451">#47451</a>) and runner-owned Triton kernel warmup (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49903">#49903</a>) that remove first-request compilation stalls.</li>
<li><strong>DeepSeek-V4 performance push</strong>: sequence parallelism (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46789">#46789</a>), ~2x kernel improvement by skipping empty c128 launches (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48957">#48957</a>), 3.4% E2E TTFT from skipping unneeded topk/router (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49486">#49486</a>), 3.9% E2E TTFT from workspace reuse (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49236">#49236</a>), 1.88x kernel from removing a redundant full kernel (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50298">#50298</a>), adaptive topk width (1.0% E2E, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50004">#50004</a>), 448 MiB GPU memory saved in the PP buffer (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50312">#50312</a>), a compact MXFP4 indexer KV cache (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48993">#48993</a>), and removal of sparse-MLA q-head padding on FlashInfer &gt;= 0.6.14 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48047">#48047</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Model Runner V2 expands to non-generative workloads</strong>: encoder-only attention (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49331">#49331</a>), sequence pooling for embedding/classification (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48791">#48791</a>), encoder token classification (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50293">#50293</a>) and token embedding (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50574">#50574</a>), BGE-M3 pooling (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50661">#50661</a>), multimodal on CPU (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50073">#50073</a>), a multi-layer MTP speculator (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48892">#48892</a>), and PCP now selects MRV2 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50034">#50034</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Resilient large-scale serving</strong>: a (simplified) fault tolerance framework for DP+EP external load-balancer deployments (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44428">#44428</a>) and async preparation for elastic EP scaling (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47288">#47288</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Disaggregation for hybrid models</strong>: NIXL P/D for hybrid MLA+SSM models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49762">#49762</a>), heterogeneous P/D block sizes for hybrid models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49612">#49612</a>), and MoRIIO heterogeneous TP&lt;-&gt;DP prefill/decode read routing (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46116">#46116</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Rust frontend grows a gRPC control plane</strong>: engine-aware health reporting (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48992">#48992</a>), abort control (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49255">#49255</a>), server and model discovery (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49491">#49491</a>), KV event source discovery (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50033">#50033</a>), plus <code>vllm-bench</code> integrated into the <code>vllm</code> CLI (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48930">#48930</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Early next-gen hardware enablement</strong>: <code>sm_107</code> target for NVIDIA Rubin (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49387">#49387</a>) with NVLink all-reduce paths on SM107 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49647">#49647</a>), and ROCm gfx1250 architecture enabled (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46516">#46516</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Model Support</h3>
<ul>
<li>Kimi K3: new model (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50000">#50000</a>) with model files and kernels (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50089">#50089</a>), Python frontend (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50093">#50093</a>), Rust frontend (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50104">#50104</a>), AttnRes kernels (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50090">#50090</a>), DeepGEMM support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50458">#50458</a>), DSpark AR fusion (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50242">#50242</a>), and optional shared-expert sharding (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50656">#50656</a>).</li>
<li>New models: Qwen3.5 text-only dense and MoE (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50210">#50210</a>), K-EXAONE-2.0-750B-A37B (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50524">#50524</a>), VaultGemma via Transformers backend (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49803">#49803</a>), jina-embeddings-v5-text-nano with EuroBERT encoder backbone (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50688">#50688</a>).</li>
<li>Inkling: llm-compressor NVFP4 weights (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49258">#49258</a>) and compressed-tensors dynamic FP8 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48876">#48876</a>).</li>
<li>Multimodal: VidCom2 video token pruning (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47750">#47750</a>), EVS for Qwen3.5 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48912">#48912</a>), ViT CUDA graph for Gemma-4 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46837">#46837</a>), Cosmos3 FP8 ModelOpt/Diffusers remapping (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48952">#48952</a>), MiniMax-M3 MSA speculative decode verification (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50032">#50032</a>) and default video processor (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50305">#50305</a>), DeepSeek-OCR-2 TTFT optimization (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49531">#49531</a>), longer max audio duration for MOSS-TD (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49403">#49403</a>).</li>
<li>Diffusion models: top_k and top_p sampling for DiffusionGemma (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45429">#45429</a>).</li>
<li>Transformers modeling backend: audio model support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39330">#39330</a>), improved <code>fx</code> tracer (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49957">#49957</a>), fused residual-add + RMSNorm compilation pass (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48757">#48757</a>), and fixes for MLA padding + grouped topk routing (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49982">#49982</a>), MQA with TP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49987">#49987</a>), and Qwen3-VL M-RoPE (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49292">#49292</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Engine Core</h3>
<ul>
<li>Warmup: new JIT warmup infrastructure (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47451">#47451</a>), runner-owned Triton kernels warmed before the first request (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49903">#49903</a>), and proper renderer warmup (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50408">#50408</a>).</li>
<li>Attention: FlashAttention 4 SM100 FP8 KV cache (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/42569">#42569</a>) and headdim-256 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/42669">#42669</a>); query replication for MLA decode under DCP for DeepSeek-V2/R1 and Kimi-K2.5 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45964">#45964</a>); masked MHA for sparse MLA prefills (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48770">#48770</a>); skip sparse indexer scoring for short dense prefills (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48407">#48407</a>); FlexAttention epilogue hook (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45841">#45841</a>) and encoder block-mask compile explosion avoided (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50339">#50339</a>); attention backends stay eligible for text-only serving of prefix-LM models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48796">#48796</a>); merge-attention context count as a runtime argument (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48739">#48739</a>); unified multi-path encoder CUDA graph support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49934">#49934</a>); encoder cache extension hooks (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48218">#48218</a>).</li>
<li>Model Runner V2: encoder-only attention (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49331">#49331</a>), sequence pooling for embedding and classification (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48791">#48791</a>), encoder token classification (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50293">#50293</a>), encoder token embedding (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50574">#50574</a>), BGE-M3 pooling (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50661">#50661</a>), multimodal on CPU (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50073">#50073</a>), multi-layer MTP speculator (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48892">#48892</a>), PCP selects MRV2 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50034">#50034</a>), attention metadata always built at capture time (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49364">#49364</a>), encoder cache profiling (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47985">#47985</a>), skipped no-op FP32 logits materialization (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47711">#47711</a>), chunked rejection sampler to avoid OOM (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48630">#48630</a>), fewer GPU&lt;-&gt;CPU syncs in hybrid Mamba (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49736">#49736</a>).</li>
<li>KV offloading: generic P2P secondary tier with peer lookup/serving (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48021">#48021</a>), per-request tier filtering with TierFilter/TierMatcher (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48123">#48123</a>), self-describing KV events with TieringOffloadingSpec (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48679">#48679</a>), pluggable eviction policies via CachePolicyFactory (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49114">#49114</a>), deduplicated replicated MLA KV in the shared CPU region (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48906">#48906</a>), single-copy MLA layout for CPUOffloadingSpec (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50301">#50301</a>), CPUOffloadingSpec moved onto SharedOffloadRegion (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50094">#50094</a>), TP-independent compact secondary identity (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49858">#49858</a>), batched C store/load for filesystem offload (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49152">#49152</a>), reliable partial-tail offload for sub-block prompts (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49502">#49502</a>), per-layer canonical KV page mappings for parallelism-agnostic offload (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48408">#48408</a>).</li>
<li>Mamba/hybrid: ReplaySSM caching for faster Mamba2 standard decode (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48018">#48018</a>), fused align-mode DS-conv state migration with num_accepted_tokens &gt; 1 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49291">#49291</a>), FlashInfer Mamba SSU algorithm selection (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50157">#50157</a>), fixed <code>/wake_up</code> crash on hybrid models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/41602">#41602</a>).</li>
<li>Spec decode: DSpark Markov head replicated across TP ranks (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49731">#49731</a>), <code>sample_from_anchor</code> loaded from speculators config (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48639">#48639</a>), earliest-completing stop string selected (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49391">#49391</a>).</li>
<li>Structured outputs: grammar advanced across the reasoning boundary with spec decode (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44993">#44993</a>).</li>
<li>Preprocessing performance: derender CPU work offloaded to the renderer thread pool (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49396">#49396</a>), raw-prompt preprocessing off the event loop in AsyncLLM (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49608">#49608</a>), multimodal preprocessing isolated on its own executor (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49524">#49524</a>), MM embeds loading deferred off the event loop (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49477">#49477</a>), parallel preprocessing within a request for online pooling (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49153">#49153</a>), videos hashed by source bytes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49607">#49607</a>), original image mode preserved in ImageIO (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49159">#49159</a>).</li>
<li>RL: weight version tagging for RL rollouts (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49040">#49040</a>), stateful trainer-send IPC (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48981">#48981</a>), vLLM config set during weight reload (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45989">#45989</a>), router replay output from the FlashInfer monolithic MoE kernel (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44214">#44214</a>).</li>
<li>Memory &amp; robustness: CuMem slept-L1 fragmentation accounting (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49208">#49208</a>), cgroup memory limits respected on all platforms (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49966">#49966</a>), fail fast when /dev/shm is too small (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48879">#48879</a>), zero-copy tensor pickling in shm_broadcast (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48442">#48442</a>), LRU hash-split skipped in free_blocks when prefix caching is off (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48017">#48017</a>), location-derived path vars excluded from torch.compile cache factors (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47573">#47573</a>), <code>CustomOp.forward_native</code> compiled for ReLU^2 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50244">#50244</a>), HF config used for HF tokenizers (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49907">#49907</a>), batch-invariant RMSNorm via pinned block size (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48391">#48391</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Hardware &amp; Performance</h3>
<ul>
<li>DeepSeek-V4: sequence parallelism (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46789">#46789</a>), ~2x kernel skipping empty c128 launches (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48957">#48957</a>), 3.4% E2E TTFT skipping topk/router in decode (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49486">#49486</a>), 3.9% E2E TTFT workspace reuse (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49236">#49236</a>), 1.88x kernel removing a redundant full kernel (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50298">#50298</a>), adaptive topk width 1.0% E2E (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50004">#50004</a>), 448 MiB GPU memory saved (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50312">#50312</a>), compact MXFP4 indexer KV cache (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48993">#48993</a>), sparse-MLA q-head padding removed for FlashInfer &gt;= 0.6.14 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48047">#48047</a>).</li>
<li>Kernels: RMSNorm uncontiguous support with 1.2–3.1x kernel improvement (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49750">#49750</a>), MoE <code>reduce_scatter</code> regression fix restoring 5% E2E throughput (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48763">#48763</a>), non-grouped bias-less topk routing dispatched to the fused path (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49618">#49618</a>), tuned LL BF16 router GEMM (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48774">#48774</a>) with warmup skipped for non-MoE models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49659">#49659</a>), Triton tensor-descriptor path for fused MoE via <code>VLLM_TRITON_USE_TD</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/42436">#42436</a>), cudagraph/DP padding skipped in topk (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48979">#48979</a>), coalesced HBM access in the Marlin INT4-FP8 AWQ preprocess kernel (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47268">#47268</a>).</li>
<li>NVIDIA next-gen: <code>sm_107</code> for Rubin (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49387">#49387</a>), NVLink all-reduce paths on SM107 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49647">#49647</a>), fixed CUDA arch detection producing kernel-less builds on SM121 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49904">#49904</a>).</li>
<li>ROCm: gfx1250 architecture enabled (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46516">#46516</a>), AITER FP8 ViT encoder attention (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49937">#49937</a>), fused shared expert for Quark DeepSeek-V4 checkpoints (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48044">#48044</a>), Quark GLM-5.2 checkpoint inference fixes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48886">#48886</a>), DSv3.2 per-decode FillFunctor launches eliminated in the sparse-MLA hot loop (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44527">#44527</a>), B-preshuffled attention FP8 projections for DSv4 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46720">#46720</a>), TML Inkling enabled (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48841">#48841</a>), tuned selective_state_update float16 config for MI325X (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50006">#50006</a>), GPT-J-style MRoPE fixed and optimized (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49906">#49906</a>), quickreduce accuracy fix in cudagraph mode (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46913">#46913</a>), cached fp32 upcast of static e8m0 weight scales (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47773">#47773</a>), batch DMA for CPU KV cache loads (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49843">#49843</a>), elastic EP scaling accuracy fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47206">#47206</a>).</li>
<li>XPU: QK Norm + RoPE fusion pass (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49394">#49394</a>), FP8 o_proj with fp8_bmm and load-time scale transpose (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48334">#48334</a>), DeepSeek-V4 fuse_index_q SYCL kernel path (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45991">#45991</a>), TD operand loads for batched MoE GEMM (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46340">#46340</a>), RMSNorm kernels unified with vllm_c (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46981">#46981</a>).</li>
<li>CPU: INT8 fused MoE kernel for Arm CPUs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48637">#48637</a>), s390x inference optimization with oneDNN INT8 GEMM (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50219">#50219</a>), GDN conv path optimized for speculative decoding (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48577">#48577</a>), granite-4 enabled (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47641">#47641</a>), FAST_EXP for Power (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49571">#49571</a>), CPU kernels bumped to the latest version (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50387">#50387</a>), macOS build fixes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49021">#49021</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50915">#50915</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Large Scale Serving &amp; Distributed</h3>
<ul>
<li>Fault tolerance framework (simplified) for DP+EP external LB deployments (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44428">#44428</a>).</li>
<li>Elastic EP: async preparation (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47288">#47288</a>) and non-contiguous weight transfer fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50641">#50641</a>).</li>
<li>P/D disaggregation: NIXL P/D for hybrid MLA+SSM models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49762">#49762</a>), NIXL heterogeneous P/D block sizes for hybrid models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49612">#49612</a>), MoRIIO heterogeneous TP&lt;-&gt;DP prefill/decode read routing (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46116">#46116</a>), optional lookup disable on PD decode (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50498">#50498</a>), prefill token ids reused on the decode chat path (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48145">#48145</a>), detokenization streaming derender (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47301">#47301</a>), NixlPush skips an extra handshake step in D-&gt;P (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49345">#49345</a>).</li>
<li>Fixes: P/D preemption race (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50297">#50297</a>), KV lease deadlines rebased onto the worker clock (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50326">#50326</a>), NIXL hybrid MLA+mamba heterogeneous TP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49297">#49297</a>), internal LB load-balancing (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49204">#49204</a>).</li>
<li>Mooncake: vectorized <code>prepare_value</code> on the KV load path (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48531">#48531</a>), full external hits re-derived on stored boundaries (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49481">#49481</a>).</li>
<li>Encoder-cache connectors: <code>has_pending_push_work</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49582">#49582</a>).</li>
<li>Communicators: process-checkpoint lifecycle hooks, starting with FlashInfer (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46877">#46877</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Quantization</h3>
<ul>
<li>New capabilities: FP4 Qutlass integration for compressed-tensors (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/43229">#43229</a>), CuTeDSL MoE for ReLU2 NVFP4 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49580">#49580</a>), MXFP8 linear support in INC (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47514">#47514</a>), AutoRound W4A16 MoE and MXFP4 linear/MoE on XPU (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47124">#47124</a>), KV quant mode for TurboQuant (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50533">#50533</a>), ModelOpt FP8 emulation on SM80 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50019">#50019</a>), <code>--linear-backend</code> honored for ModelOpt W4A16 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50273">#50273</a>).</li>
<li>Checkpoints: compressed-tensors support for DeepSeek-V4 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/41276">#41276</a>) and Kimi-K3 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50500">#50500</a>); <code>find_matched_target</code> prioritizes fused-name matches (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49483">#49483</a>).</li>
<li>MoE refactor: FusedMoE renamed to FusedMoEFactory (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44941">#44941</a>), MoeWNA16 migrated to the MK oracle scheme (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44120">#44120</a>), Quark w8a8-int8 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46765">#46765</a>) and MXFP4 <code>aiter</code>/<code>emulation</code> backends (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49348">#49348</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48949">#48949</a>) moved to kernel abstractions, CT WNA16 Marlin/MoE methods merged (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44570">#44570</a>), Quark W4A8 (INT4-FP8) MoE CI coverage (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48050">#48050</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>API &amp; Frontend</h3>
<ul>
<li>Rust frontend: gRPC control plane with engine-aware health reporting (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48992">#48992</a>), abort control RPC (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49255">#49255</a>), server and model discovery (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49491">#49491</a>), and KV event source discovery (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50033">#50033</a>); <code>vllm-bench</code> integrated into <code>vllm-rs</code> and the <code>vllm</code> CLI (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48930">#48930</a>) with opt-in Rust delegation for <code>vllm bench serve</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50081">#50081</a>); zero-copy multimodal tensor slicing (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48781">#48781</a>), multimodal tensors in auxiliary frames (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49341">#49341</a>), <code>--limit-mm-per-prompt</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49604">#49604</a>), ordinary-text tokenizer encoding (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49992">#49992</a>).</li>
<li>APIs: Cohere chat v2 API support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47189">#47189</a>), <code>cache_salt</code> in the Anthropic Messages API (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49498">#49498</a>), strict tool calling and constrained decoding for GPT-OSS Harmony (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45560">#45560</a>), unified engine-based Mistral parser for reasoning and tool calls (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48947">#48947</a>), <code>stream_interval</code> exposed as a per-request sampling param (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49754">#49754</a>), additional sampling parameters for the translation API (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45839">#45839</a>), <code>diarized_json</code> for MOSS-Transcribe-Diarize (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48543">#48543</a>), cumulative speech-to-text chunk timestamps (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/41131">#41131</a>).</li>
<li>Multimodal: mm hash algorithm selection via CLI (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49686">#49686</a>), configurable PyNvVideoCodec decoder concurrency (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49753">#49753</a>), RFC 2397 parameters accepted in base64 data URLs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48973">#48973</a>).</li>
<li>UX &amp; validation: standardized request error handling with a VLLMError hierarchy (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49665">#49665</a>), reduced startup log noise (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50590">#50590</a>), incompatible nested runtime overrides rejected (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49247">#49247</a>), improved data-parallel launch validation (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49124">#49124</a>), DCP topology validation (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49777">#49777</a>), 400 instead of 500 for non-numeric logprobs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49144">#49144</a>), bare Inkling text preserved in Python and Rust parsers (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50403">#50403</a>).</li>
<li>Benchmarks: probe requests in <code>vllm bench serve</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49611">#49611</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Dependencies</h3>
<ul>
<li>PyTorch 2.13.0, torchvision 0.28.0, Triton 3.7.1 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48155">#48155</a>); torch 2.13 for XPU (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48677">#48677</a>) and CPU (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50412">#50412</a>).</li>
<li>Transformers 5.14.1 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49223">#49223</a>), FlashInfer 0.6.15 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48914">#48914</a>) then 0.6.16.post3 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50892">#50892</a>), AITER 0.1.16.post5 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48683">#48683</a>) then 0.1.19 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49361">#49361</a>), NCCL 2.30.7 enabling DeepEPv2 in the vllm/vllm-openai image (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45321">#45321</a>), tpu-inference v0.25.0 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49431">#49431</a>) then v0.26.0 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50522">#50522</a>), Helion 1.4.0 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50307">#50307</a>), NIXL and UCX upgraded on ROCm (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49251">#49251</a>).</li>
<li>Build: vllm-flash-attn bumped to a C++20-compatible commit for torch-nightly (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49326">#49326</a>), ABI-stable FA2 build pin (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50474">#50474</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Deprecations &amp; Removals</h3>
<ul>
<li>Models removed: Plamo2 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49729">#49729</a>), Ouro (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49786">#49786</a>).</li>
<li>Removed the no-longer-supported <code>max_num_partial_prefills</code> and <code>max_long_partial_prefills</code> arguments (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49244">#49244</a>).</li>
</ul>

### [v0.26.0](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/releases/tag/v0.26.0)

**来源**: vLLM GitHub Releases

**摘要**: <h1>vLLM v0.26.0 Release Notes</h1>
<h2>Highlights</h2>
<p>This release features 411 commits from 212 contributors (61 new)!</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>New Inkling model family</strong> with a full support stack: base modeling (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48799">#48799</a>), piecewise CUDA graph support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48822">#48822</a>), Hopper FA4 relative attention (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48858">#48858</a>), MTP=1 speculative decoding (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48869">#48869</a>), LoRA (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48884">#48884</a>), and standard ModelOpt NVFP4 quantization (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48990">#48990</a>).</li>
<li><strong>DeepSeek-V4 performance push</strong> across vendors: a specialized routing kernel (2.94% E2E TPOT, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48660">#48660</a>), <code>fused_topk_bias</code> (1.5–2x kernel, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47463">#47463</a>), and redundant repeat/copy removal (1.8% E2E TPOT, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48137">#48137</a>), plus ROCm two-stage compressor for HCA prefill (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47718">#47718</a>), sparse decode/prefill optimizations (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48519">#48519</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48788">#48788</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46275">#46275</a>), and DSpark speculative decoding on AMD (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47419">#47419</a>) and XPU (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47677">#47677</a>).</li>
<li><strong>fp32 <code>lm_head</code> for generation models via <code>head_dtype</code></strong> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48390">#48390</a>), extended to the LoRA path (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48525">#48525</a>) and given a ROCm <code>torch.mm</code> fast path (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48688">#48688</a>), improving accuracy for generation heads.</li>
<li><strong>Flexible attention backends</strong>: the attention backend can now be selected per KV-cache group (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48012">#48012</a>), and sliding-window support is now an explicit backend capability (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48011">#48011</a>) — improving support for hybrid models.</li>
<li><strong>KV offloading &amp; tiered secondary storage</strong> matured substantially: offloading metrics (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45958">#45958</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47666">#47666</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47679">#47679</a>), tier-owned event handling (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46544">#46544</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47923">#47923</a>), object-store secondary tier with workload identity (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47063">#47063</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47274">#47274</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48150">#48150</a>), DP-replica-aware tiering (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47987">#47987</a>), and encoder-cache (EC) connectors including CPU offloading (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/42433">#42433</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47423">#47423</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Rust frontend</strong> gained multimodal video (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47959">#47959</a>) and audio (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48554">#48554</a>), a Seed-OSS tool parser (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47741">#47741</a>), and a native <code>vllm-bench</code> port (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48107">#48107</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Transformers 5.13.0</strong> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47867">#47867</a>) with more models migrated to the Transformers modeling backend: Olmo/Olmo2 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48100">#48100</a>), MistralLarge3 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48153">#48153</a>), and HunyuanVL (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47872">#47872</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Model Support</h3>
<ul>
<li>New models: Inkling family (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48799">#48799</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48822">#48822</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48858">#48858</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48869">#48869</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48884">#48884</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48990">#48990</a>), BertForMaskedLM (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48463">#48463</a>), RobertaForTokenClassification / XLMRobertaForTokenClassification (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47991">#47991</a>), LongCat-Flash-Lite n-gram embedding (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47857">#47857</a>), Cosmos3 Edge Reasoner (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48291">#48291</a>) and Cosmos3-Super registration (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48211">#48211</a>), TranslateGemma-12b-it (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/41599">#41599</a>).</li>
<li>Transformers backend migrations: Olmo/Olmo2 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48100">#48100</a>), MistralLarge3 to AutoWeightsLoader (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48153">#48153</a>), HunyuanVL native transformers processor for transformers 5.13 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47872">#47872</a>).</li>
<li>GLM5.2: migrate MoE sequence-parallel support to the non-torch-compiled path (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47881">#47881</a>).</li>
<li>LoRA: FlashInfer MoE LoRA for BF16 models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48632">#48632</a>), LoRA for tower/connector in LlavaNextVideo (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48594">#48594</a>), fp32 <code>lm_head</code> on the LoRA path (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48525">#48525</a>), optimized <code>TrtLlmLoRAExperts</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48759">#48759</a>).</li>
<li>Multimodal: automatic fallback to ViT data parallelism when TP is unavailable (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49046">#49046</a>).</li>
<li>Fixes: correct pooling scores for chunked prefill under <code>torch.compile</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48901">#48901</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Engine Core</h3>
<ul>
<li>fp32 <code>lm_head</code> for generation models via <code>head_dtype</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48390">#48390</a>); lower memory for capturing large CUDA graph sizes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48483">#48483</a>); opt-in persistence and reuse of the memory-profiling result across boots (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47388">#47388</a>); improved InstantTensor loading (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46868">#46868</a>).</li>
<li>Attention: select a different attention backend per KV-cache group (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48012">#48012</a>); sliding-window as an explicit backend capability (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48011">#48011</a>); KV-cache layout refactor packing K/V into the content dim across backends (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44455">#44455</a>); MRV2 virtual-batch PCP for MLA (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46570">#46570</a>).</li>
<li>Speculative decoding: runtime draft weight update (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46725">#46725</a>), hybrid (SWA + full attention) DFlash drafters (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47914">#47914</a>), SWA support for qwen-eagle3 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47568">#47568</a>), Gemma4-12B DSpark draft model (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47216">#47216</a>), DSv4 DSpark on AMD (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47419">#47419</a>), separate <code>kv_cache_dtype</code> for <code>speculative_config</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48787">#48787</a>).</li>
<li>KV offloading: basic offloading metrics (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45958">#45958</a>), split CPU cache usage into read/write gauges (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47666">#47666</a>) and tiering-lookup-delay into sync/async histograms (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47679">#47679</a>), tier-owned event handling and BlockStored events (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46544">#46544</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47923">#47923</a>), object-store secondary tier with workload identity (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47063">#47063</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47274">#47274</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48150">#48150</a>), DP-replica-aware tiering (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47987">#47987</a>), <code>blocks_per_chunk</code> config for heterogeneous KV groups (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48878">#48878</a>), P2P default host/port env vars (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47636">#47636</a>).</li>
<li>Caching: partial prefix-cache hit for hybrid models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46384">#46384</a>), selective hybrid cache retention (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47782">#47782</a>), report prefix-cache-reused blocks in full report mode (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45261">#45261</a>).</li>
<li>Reasoning: optimize TPOT for thinking budget when used with speculative decoding (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46662">#46662</a>).</li>
<li>RLHF: stateful trainer-send abstractions (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48042">#48042</a>).</li>
<li>Fixes: host memory leak from undrained <code>new_block_ids</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44490">#44490</a>), DSv3.2 + MTP + sequence-parallel accuracy (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48036">#48036</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Hardware &amp; Performance</h3>
<ul>
<li>DeepSeek-V4: specialized routing kernel (2.94% E2E TPOT, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48660">#48660</a>), <code>fused_topk_bias</code> 1.5–2x (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47463">#47463</a>), redundant repeat/copy removal (1.8% TPOT, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48137">#48137</a>).</li>
<li>MoE router GEMMs: BF16x3 router GEMM (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47973">#47973</a>), FP32 router GEMV (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48335">#48335</a>), generic CuteDSL LL BF16 router GEMM (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/42562">#42562</a>); TRTLLM BF16 MoE modular kernel (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45182">#45182</a>); write FlashInfer combine into final output (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47156">#47156</a>).</li>
<li>Qwen: fuse more RMSNorm + all-reduce in Qwen3.5 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46998">#46998</a>), replace MoE all-reduce with reduce-scatter (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47006">#47006</a>), Qwen3.5 H20 optimization (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48350">#48350</a>), expand Triton warmup coverage (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47546">#47546</a>).</li>
<li>MLA: dense MHA path for short sparse-MLA sequences (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47327">#47327</a>); MiniMax-M3 long-context decode indexer on sm100 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48582">#48582</a>).</li>
<li>Kernels: CUDA kernel for ReLUSquaredActivation / relu^2 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39058">#39058</a>), Helion kernel lazy registration (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48264">#48264</a>), vectorize <code>_copy_mamba_state_block</code> to uint64 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48110">#48110</a>), stop upcasting logits to fp32 in the sampler (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48641">#48641</a>).</li>
<li>ROCm: fp32 <code>head_dtype</code> <code>torch.mm</code> fast path (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48688">#48688</a>), DSv4 two-stage compressor kernel (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47718">#47718</a>), sparse decode/prefill optimizations (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48519">#48519</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48788">#48788</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46275">#46275</a>), DSv3.2 sparse MLA KV-split heuristic (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46832">#46832</a>) and MTP CUDA-graph mode (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45149">#45149</a>), MXFP8 GEMM for MiniMax-M3 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46117">#46117</a>), AITER sparse paged attention + spec decode for MiniMax-M3 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47287">#47287</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47984">#47984</a>), MiniMax-M2 fused QK-norm + all-reduce via AITER (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44849">#44849</a>), HybridW4A16 linear kernel (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40977">#40977</a>), Qwen3-30B-A3B QK-Norm+RoPE+KV runtime fusion (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/42749">#42749</a>).</li>
<li>XPU: batch-invariant kernels (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/41934">#41934</a>), HND KV layout support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47975">#47975</a>), DSpark spec decode for DSv4 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47677">#47677</a>), nightly/release image publishing (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47880">#47880</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48126">#48126</a>).</li>
<li>CPU: DFlash speculative decoding for GDN models on CPU (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46090">#46090</a>), s390x NUMA topology (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40714">#40714</a>), native macOS arm64 CPU wheel builds (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48289">#48289</a>); POWER VSX math function optimization (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47321">#47321</a>) and IBM Power docker builds using prebuilt wheels (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46017">#46017</a>).</li>
<li>Distributed fusion: FlashInfer MNNVL all-reduce RMS quant fusion (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48064">#48064</a>).</li>
<li>Build/autotune: arm64 Blackwell SM10x/SM110 image builds (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48041">#48041</a>); skip CuTeDSL fp4_gemm autotuning by default (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48268">#48268</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Large Scale Serving &amp; Distributed</h3>
<ul>
<li>Decode Context Parallel (DCP): hybrid attention support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40996">#40996</a>), DCP + Eagle for Tokenspeed MLA backends (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48180">#48180</a>).</li>
<li>PD disaggregation: NIXL pipeline-parallel prefill in push mode (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45880">#45880</a>).</li>
<li>Encoder-cache connectors: EC transfer params (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/42433">#42433</a>) and CPU-offloading EC connector (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47423">#47423</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Quantization</h3>
<ul>
<li>Humming w[2-7]a[4,8] weight-only inference with compressed-tensors (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46390">#46390</a>); int4 quantization for the emulation MoE backend (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48451">#48451</a>); INT2 XPU weight-only quant linear (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47521">#47521</a>).</li>
<li>NVFP4/MXFP4: <code>nvfp4_per_token</code> online MoE quantization (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48538">#48538</a>), CuTe-DSL FlashInfer MXFP4 quantization (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48417">#48417</a>); bounded peak memory when repacking FP4 MoE weights for Marlin (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47851">#47851</a>) and for NVFP4 MoE weight loading (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46276">#46276</a>).</li>
<li>MLA: <code>kv_cache_dtype_skip_layers</code> support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47309">#47309</a>).</li>
<li>ROCm: HybridW4A16 linear kernel (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40977">#40977</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>API &amp; Frontend</h3>
<ul>
<li>Rust frontend: multimodal video (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47959">#47959</a>) and audio (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48554">#48554</a>), Seed-OSS tool parser (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47741">#47741</a>), native <code>vllm-bench</code> port (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48107">#48107</a>), <code>continue_final_message</code> handling with renderer sentinel (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47844">#47844</a>).</li>
<li>OpenAI compatibility: <code>bad_words</code> in <code>/v1/completions</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46793">#46793</a>), expose <code>logprob_token_ids</code> on Python OpenAI endpoints (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/43463">#43463</a>), <code>include_reasoning</code> param for non-Harmony models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44301">#44301</a>), populate <code>num_cache_creation_tokens</code> on Messages responses (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48535">#48535</a>).</li>
<li>Endpoint plugins framework (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47454">#47454</a>); <code>/abort_requests</code> on the RLHF dev API router (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47173">#47173</a>); Deepstream video decoding backend (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/42424">#42424</a>); overlap preprocessing and computation for pooling models in offline inference (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47699">#47699</a>).</li>
<li>UX: human-readable integers for more CLI args (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47608">#47608</a>), CuTeDSL compilation progress bar (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48881">#48881</a>), expanded GPU profiler config scope/annotations (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37524">#37524</a>), log worker exit code when a process dies unexpectedly (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38641">#38641</a>).</li>
<li>Stability/correctness: handle grammar compilation failures without crashing the engine (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47312">#47312</a>), fix logprobs token-string collision from SentencePiece spaces (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48674">#48674</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Security</h3>
<ul>
<li>Replace diskcache to eliminate pickle deserialization (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44549">#44549</a>).</li>
<li>Fix a concurrent sparse-invariant race that bypassed CVE remediation (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48583">#48583</a>).</li>
<li>Add resource-bounds validation to derender endpoints (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47260">#47260</a>); sanitize server file paths from validation error responses (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46415">#46415</a>); bound the completion prompt list to prevent unbounded engine fan-out (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47845">#47845</a>); guard lm-format-enforcer regex compilation with a timeout (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47595">#47595</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Dependencies</h3>
<ul>
<li>Transformers 5.13.0 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47867">#47867</a>), FlashInfer 0.6.14 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47669">#47669</a>), NIXL 1.3.1 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47559">#47559</a>), tpu-inference v0.24.0 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47835">#47835</a>), nvidia-cutlass-dsl 4.6.0 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47442">#47442</a>), vllm_xpu_kernels v0.1.11.1 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48942">#48942</a>).</li>
<li>FlashAttention 3 pinned to the torch stable-ABI commit (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47995">#47995</a>); ABI-stable FlashMLA build (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48174">#48174</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Deprecations &amp; Removals</h3>
<ul>
<li>Models removed: TeleChat (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47989">#47989</a>), Persimmon and Fuyu (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48096">#48096</a>).</li>
</ul>

### [v0.26.0rc1](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/releases/tag/v0.26.0rc1)

**来源**: vLLM GitHub Releases

**摘要**: <p>v0.26.0rc1</p>

---

### [v0.25.1](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/releases/tag/v0.25.1)

**来源**: vLLM GitHub Releases

**摘要**: <h1>vLLM v0.25.1</h1>
<h2>Highlights</h2>
<p>This release features 2 commits from 2 contributors (1 new)!</p>
<p>v0.25.1 is a patch release containing two targeted bug fixes on top of v0.25.0.</p>
<h3>Bug Fixes</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Avoid blocking model launching when no system FFmpeg is available for TorchCodec</strong> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47888">#47888</a>). Previously <code>import torchcodec</code> raised a <code>RuntimeError</code> at import time when system FFmpeg was missing, which blocked startup (e.g. <code>vllm serve Qwen/Qwen3-VL-2B-Instruct</code>) even when TorchCodec was not in use. The error is now deferred to runtime so it only surfaces if TorchCodec is actually needed.</li>
<li><strong>Guard mixed-dtype allreduce RMSNorm quant fusions</strong> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48330">#48330</a>). The fused FlashInfer allreduce + RMSNorm + static-quantization patterns could match graphs where the activation and RMSNorm weight dtypes differ (e.g. a BF16 residual stream with an FP32 Gemma/Qwen-style RMSNorm weight in NVFP4 models), corrupting the hidden state and producing garbage output such as repeated <code>!!!!!</code> tokens. A dtype-match guard now routes incompatible mixed-dtype graphs to the safe path, while same-dtype models retain the full allreduce + RMSNorm + quant fusion.</li>
</ul>
<h2>Contributors</h2>
<p><a class="user-mention notranslate" href="https://github.com/Isotr0py">@Isotr0py</a>, <a class="user-mention notranslate" href="https://github.com/hugo-cen">@hugo-cen</a></p>
<h2>New Contributors</h2>
<ul>
<li><a class="user-mention notranslate" href="https://github.com/hugo-cen">@hugo-cen</a> made their first contribution in <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48330">#48330</a></li>
</ul>
